tat kim etena bahunā hā tāteti ca kiṃ punaḥ|| 6||
vilāpenātra yat kṛtyaṃ tadeṣo 'atra karomyaham|
yadyahaṃ tasya raktena dehotthena vapuṣmataḥ|
na karomi gurostṛptiṃ tat pravekṣye hutāśanam|| 7||
tacchoṇitenodakakarma tasya māṃsena samyagdvijabhojanaṃ ca|
kuryāṃ pitustasya ca piṇḍadānaṃ na cet pravekṣyāmi hutāśanaṃ tat|| 8||
sāhāyyam asyāsuradevayakṣa- gandharvavidyādharasiddhasaṃghāḥ|
kurvanti cet tānapi cāstrapūgairbhasmīkaromyeṣa ruṣā sametaḥ|| 8||
niḥśūram ādhārmikam apraśastaṃ taṃ dākṣiṇātyaṃ samare nihatya|
bhokṣye tato 'ahaṃ pṛthivīṃ ca kṛtsnāṃ vahniṃ pravekṣyāmyanihatya taṃ vā|| 10||
sudurmatiṃ tāpasavṛddhaghātinaṃ vanasthagaṃ sādhuvidhiṃ vidagdham|
hantāham adyākhilabandhumitra- padātihastyaśvabalaiḥ sametam|| 11||
eṣo 'aham ādāya dhanuḥ sakhaḍgo rathī tathaivāribalaṃ sametya|
karomi vai yat kadanaṃ samastāḥ paśyantu me devagaṇāḥ sametāḥ|| 12||
yo yaḥ sahāyo bhavitādya tasya mayā sametasya raṇāya bhūyaḥ|
tasyaiva niḥśeṣakulakṣayāya samudyato 'ahaṃ nijabāhusainyaḥ|| 13||
yadi kuliśakaro 'asmin saṃyuge devarājaḥ
pitṛpatiratha cograṃ daṇḍam udyamya kopāt|
dhanapativaruṇārkā rakṣituṃ taṃ yatante
niśitaśaravaraughairghātayiṣye tathāpi|| 14||
niyatamatiradoṣaḥ kānanākhaṇḍalaukāḥ
nipatitaphalabhakṣaḥ sarvabhūteṣu maitraḥ|
prabhavati mayi putre hiṃsito yena tātaḥ
piśitarudhiratṛptāstasya santvadya gṛdhrāḥ|| 15||
iti śrīmārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe damacarite dvātriṃśadadhikaśatatamo 'adhyāyaḥ|| 132||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca|
damena rājñā ityukte pituḥ śatruḥ palāyitaḥ|
matpitā tāpaso 'anyaśca tapyatāṃ nirbhayaṃ mahat|
palāyanaparān dṛṣṭvā kiṃcinnoktaṃ damena tān|| 1||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca|
etat sarvam upākhyānaṃ dharmyaṃ svargāpavargadam|
yaḥ śṛṇoti paṭhedvāpi siddhaṃ tasya samīhitam|| 2||
ādhivyādhijaduḥkhena kadācinnābhiyujyate|
brahmahatyādipāpebhyo mucyate nātra saṃśayaḥ|| 3||
santaḥ sujanamitrāṇi bhavanti hitabuddhayaḥ|
nārayaḥ sambhaviṣyanti dasyavo vā kadācana|| 4||
sadartho miṣṭabhogī ca durbhikṣairnāvasīdati|
paradāraparadravya- parahiṃsādikilbiṣaiḥ|| 5||
mucyate 'anekaduḥkhebhyo nityaṃ caivoragottama|
ṛddhirvṛddhiḥ smṛtiḥ śāntiḥ śrīḥ puṣṭistuṣṭireva ca|
nityaṃ tasya bhavedvipra yaḥ śṛṇoti kathām imām|| 6||
mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇam etadakhilaṃ śṛṇvannaśocyaḥ pumān
yo vā samyagudīrayedrasamayaṃ śocyo na so 'api dvija|
yogajñānaviśuddhasiddhisahitaḥ svargādiloke 'apyasau
śakrādyaiśca surādibhiḥ parivṛtaḥ svarge sadā pūjyate|| 7||
purāṇam etacchrutvā ca jñānavijñānasaṃyutam|
vimānavaram āruhya svargaloke mahīyate|| 8||
iti śrīmārkaṇḍeyapurāṇaṃ samāptam|| 137||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca|
iti pratijñāya tadā nariṣyantasuto damaḥ|
kopāmarṣavivṛttākṣaḥ śmaśrum āvṛtya pāṇinā|| 1||
hā hato 'asmīti pitaraṃ dhyātvā daivaṃ vinindya ca|
provāca mantriṇaḥ sarvān ānināya purohitam|| 2||
dama uvāca|
yadatra kṛtyaṃ tadbrūta tāte prāpte surālayam|
śrutaṃ bhavadbhiryat proktaṃ tena śūdratapasvinā|| 3||
vṛddhastapasvī sa nṛpo vānaprasthavrate sthitaḥ|
maunavrātadharo 'aśastro manmātrā cendrasenayā|| 4||
proktaṃ saṃsṛṣṭayā svātmyādyāthātathyaṃ vapuṣmate|
tenāpi khaḍgam ākṛṣya jaṭāṃ savyena pāṇinā|| 5||
dhṛtvā jaghāna duṣṭātmā lokanātham anāthavat|
mātā ca saṃdiśya hi māṃ dhikchabdaṃ bruvatī satī|| 6||
mandabhāgyaṃ ca niḥśrīkaṃ praviṣṭā havyavāhanam|
tam āliṅgya nariṣyantaṃ prayātā tridaśalāyam|| 7||
so 'aham adya kariṣyāmi yanme māturudīritam|
hastyaśva- rathapādātaṃ sainyaṃ ca parikalpyatām|| 8||
aniryāpya piturvairam ahatvā pitṛghātakam|
akṛtvā ca vaco māturjīvituṃ kim ihotsahe|| 9||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca|
mantriṇastadvacaḥ śrutvā hā hetyuktvā tathā ca tat|
kṛtavanto vimanasaḥ sabhṛtyabalavāhanāḥ|| 10||
niryayuḥ saparīvārāḥ puraskṛtya damaṃ nṛpam|
gṛhītvā cāśiṣo viprāstrikālajñāḥ purodhasaḥ|| 11||
ahirāḍiva niḥśvasya damaḥ prāyādvapuṣmatam|
sīmāpālādisāmantān nighnan yāmyāṃ diśaṃ tvarā|| 12||
nirīkṣya taṃ samāyāntaṃ vapuṣmān marṣapūritaḥ|
saṃkrandanasutenāpi damo jñāto vapuṣmatā|
āyātaḥ saparīvāraḥ sāmātyaḥ saparicchadaḥ|| 13||
akampitena manasā sasainyāni dideśa ha|
dūtaṃ ca preṣayāmāsa nirgamya nagarādbahiḥ|| 14||
tvaṃ śīghrataram āgaccha nariṣyantaḥ pratīkṣate|
sabhārya- kṣatrabandho tvaṃ samāyāhi mamāntikam|| 15||
ime madbāhunirmuktāḥ śitā bāṇāḥ pipāsitāḥ|
bhittvā śarīraṃ saṃgrāme pāsyanti rudhiraṃ tava|| 16||
śrutvā damastu tat sarvaṃ dūtaproktaṃ yayau tvaran|
smṛtvā pratijñāṃ pūrvoktāṃ niḥśvasannurago yathā|| 17||
āhūta- samare caiva pumān senā vikatthitā|
tato yuddham atīvāsīddamasya ca vapuṣmataḥ|| 18||
rathī ca rathinā nāgī nāginā hayinā hayī|
ayudhyanta ca viprarṣe tadyuddhaṃ tumulaṃ hyabhūt|| 19||
paśyatāṃ sarvadevānāṃ siddhagandharvarakṣasām|
cakampe vasudhā brahman yudhyamāne dame yudhi|| 20||
na gajo na rathī nāśvastasya bāṇasahastu yaḥ|
tato damena yuyudhe senādhyakṣo vapuṣmataḥ|| 21||
hṛdi vivyādha ca dama iṣuṇāgādyamāntikam|
tasmin nipatite sainyaṃ palāyanaparaṃ hyabhūt|| 22||
asvāmikaṃ tataḥ prāha damaḥ śatruṃdamastathā|
kva yāsi duṣṭa pitaraṃ ghātayitvā tapasvinam|| 23||
aśastraṃ ca tapasyantaṃ kṣatriyo 'asi nivartatām|
tato nivṛtya sa damaṃ yodhayāmāsa sānujaḥ|| 24||
sa putraḥ saha sambādhaṃ bāndhavairyuyudhe rathī|
tataḥ śarāsanānmukta- bāṇairvyāptāstato diśaḥ|| 25||
damaṃ ca sarathaṃ cāśu śarajālairapūrayat|
tataḥ pitṛvadhotthena kopena sa damastathā|| 26||
ciccheda tāñcharāṃsteṣāṃ vivyādhānyaiśca tānapi|
ekenaikena bāṇena sapta putrāṃstathā dvija|| 27||
sambandhibāndhavān mitrān nināya yamasādanam|
vapuṣmān sa rathī krodhānnihatātmajabāndhavaḥ|| 28||
yuyudhe ca sa tenājau śarai rāśīviṣopamaiḥ|
ciccheda tasya tān bāṇān sa damaśca mahāmune|| 29||
yuyudhāte ca saṃrabdhau parasparajayaiṣiṇau|
parasparaśarāghāta- vicchinnadhanuṣau tvarā|| 30||
gṛhītakhaḍgāvuttīrya cikrīḍāte mahābalau|
damaḥ kṣaṇaṃ nṛpaṃ dhyātvā pitaraṃ nihataṃ vane|| 31||
keśeṣu ākṛṣya cākramya nipātya dharaṇītale|
śirodharāyāṃ pādena bhujam udyamya cābravīt|| 32||
paśyantu devatāḥ sarvā mānuṣāḥ pannagāḥ khagāḥ|
pāṭyamānaṃ ca hṛdayaṃ kṣatrabandhorvapuṣmataḥ|| 33||
evam uktvā ca sa damo hṛdayaṃ ca vyadārayat|
pātu- kāmaśca sa suraiḥ kṣatajena nivāritaḥ|| 34||
tataścakāra tātasya raktenaivodakakriyām|
ānṛṇyaṃ prāpya sa pituḥ punaḥ prāyāt svamandiram|| 35||
vapuṣmataśca māṃsena piṇḍadānaṃ cakāra ha|
brāhmaṇān bhojayāmāsa rakṣaḥkulasamudbhavān|| 36||
evaṃvidhā hi rājāno babhūvuḥ sūryavaṃśajāḥ|
anye 'api sudhiyaḥ śūrā yajvinaḥ śāstrakovidāḥ|| 37||
vedāntapāragāstāṃśca na saṃkhyātum ihotsahe|
eteṣāṃ caritaṃ śrutvā naraḥ pāpaiḥ pramucyate|| 38||
śrīmārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe damacarite vapuṣmadvadho nāma trayastriṃśadadhikaśatatamo 'adhyāyaḥ|| 133||
pakṣiṇa ūcuḥ|
evam uktvā jaimineyaṃ mārkaṇḍeyo mahāmuniḥ|
visṛjya krauṣṭukimuniṃ cakre mādhyāhnikīṃ kriyām|| 1||
asmābhiśca śrutaṃ yasmādyat te proktaṃ mahāmune|
anādi- siddham etaddhi purā proktaṃ svayambhuvā|| 2||
mārkaṇḍeyāya munaye yat te 'asmābhirudāhṛtam|
puṇyaṃ pavitram āyuṣyaṃ dharmakāmārthasiddhidam|| 3||
paṭhatāṃ śṛṇvatāṃ sadyaḥ sarvapāpapramocanam|
ādāveva kṛtā ye ca praśnāścatvāra eva hi|| 4||
pituḥ putrasya saṃvādastathā sṛṣṭiḥ svayambhuvaḥ|
tathā manūnāṃ sthitayo rājñāṃ ca caritaṃ mune|| 5||
asmābhiretat te proktaṃ kim adya śrotum icchasi|
etān sarvān naraḥ śrutvā paṭhate vā sabhāsu ca|| 6||
vidhūya sarvapāpāni brahmaṇo 'ante layaṃ vrajet|
aṣṭādaśapurāṇāni yāni prāha pitāmahaḥ|| 7||
teṣāṃ tu saptamaṃ jñeyaṃ mārkaṇḍeyaṃ siviśrutam|
brāhmaṃ pādmaṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ ca śaivaṃ bhāgavataṃ tathā|| 8||
tathānyannāradīyaṃ ca mārkaṇḍeyaṃ ca saptamam|
āgneyam aṣṭamaṃ proktaṃ bhaviṣyaṃ navamaṃ tathā|| 9||
daśamaṃ brahmavaivartaṃ laiṅgam ekādaśaṃ smṛtam|
vārāhaṃ dvādaśaṃ proktaṃ skāndam atra trayodaśam|| 10||
caturdaśaṃ vāmanaṃ ca kaurmaṃ pañcadaśaṃ tathā|
mātsyaṃ ca gāruḍaṃ caiva brahmāṇḍaṃ ca tataḥ param|| 11||
aṣṭādaśapurāṇānāṃ nāmadheyāni yaḥ paṭhet|
trisaṃdhyaṃ japate nityaṃ so 'aśvamedhaphalaṃ labhet|| 12||
sargaśca pratisargaśca vaṃśo manvantarāṇi ca|
vaṃśānucaritaṃ caiva purāṇaṃ pañcalakṣaṇam|| 13||
catuḥpraśnasamopetaṃ purāṇaṃ hyetaduttamam|
śrutvā punaśca te pāpaṃ kalpakoṭiśataiḥ kṛtam|| 14||
brahmahatyādipāpāni yānyanyānyaśubhāni ca|
tāni sarvāṇi naśyanti tṛṇaṃ vātahataṃ yathā|| 15||
puṣkare dānajaṃ puṇyaṃ śravaṇādasya jāyate|
sarvavedādhikaphalaṃ samāptyā cādhigacchati|| 16||
yaḥ śrāvayet pūjayet taṃ yathā devaṃ pitāmaham|
gandhapuṣpaistathā vastrairbrāhmaṇānāṃ ca tarpaṇaiḥ|| 17||
yathā śaktyā ca dātavyaṃ nṛpairgrāmādivāhanam|
etat purāṇam akhilaṃ vedārthairupabṛṃhitam|
dharmaśāstraikanilayaṃ śrutvā sarvārtham āpnuyāt|| 18||
śrutvā purāṇam akhilaṃ vyāsaṃ sampūjayedbudhaḥ|
dharmārthakāmamokṣāṇāṃ yathoktaphalahetave|| 19||
dadyādgāṃ gurave svarṇa- vastrālaṃkārasaṃyutām|
śravaṇasya phalāvāptyai dānaiḥ saṃtoṣayedgurum|| 20||
apūjya pāṭhakartāraṃ ślokam ekaṃ ca śṛṇoti yaḥ|
nāsau puṇyam avāpnoti śāstracoraḥ smṛto hi saḥ|| 21||
na tasya devāḥ prīṇanti pitaro naiva putrakān|
dattaṃ śrāddhaṃ tathecchanti tīrthasnānaphalaṃ na ca|| 22||
labheta śāstracoraśca nindāṃ sajjanasaṃsadi|
avajñayā na śrotavyaṃ śāstram etadvicakṣaṇaiḥ|| 23||
paṭhyamāne tvavajñāte sādhubhiḥ śāstra uttame|
mūko bhavati janmāni sapta mūrkhaḥ prajāyate|| 24||
śrutvā tat pūjayedyastu purāṇaṃ saptamaṃ punaḥ|
sarvapāpavinirmuktaḥ punātyeva nijaṃ kulam|| 25||
pūto yāti na saṃdeho viṣṇulokaṃ sanātanam|
cyutastataḥ punarnaiva sa bhaviṣyati mānavaḥ|| 26||
purāṇaśravaṇādeva paraṃ yogam avāpnuyāt|
nāstikāya na dātavyaṃ vṛṣale vedanindake|| 27||
gurudvijātinindāya tathā bhagnavratāya ca|
mātāpitrornindakāya vedaśāstrādinindine|| 28||
bhinnamaryādine caiva tathā vai jñātikopine|
eteṣāṃ naiva dātavyaṃ prāṇaiḥ kaṇṭhagatairapi|| 29||
lobhādvā yadi vā mohādbhayādvāpi viśeṣataḥ|
p.athedvā pāṭhayedvāpi sa gacchennarakaṃ dhruvam|| 30||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca|
etat sarvam upākhyānaṃ dharmyaṃ svargāpavargadam|
yaḥ śṛṇoti paṭhedvāpi siddhaṃ tasya samīhitam|| 31||
ādhivyādhijaduḥkhena kadācinnābhiyujyate|
brahmahatyādipāpebhyo mucyate nātra saṃśayaḥ|| 32||
santaḥ svajanamitrāṇi bhavanti hitabuddhayaḥ|
nārayaḥ sambhaviṣyanti dasyavo vā kadācana|| 33||
sadartho miṣṭabhogī ca durbhikṣairnāvasīdati|
paradāraparadravya- parahiṃsādikilbiṣaiḥ|| 34||
mucyate 'anekaduḥkhebhyo nityaṃ caiva dvijottama|
ṛddhirvṛddhiḥ smṛtiḥ śāntiḥ śrīḥ puṣṭistuṣṭireva ca|
nityaṃ tasya bhavedvipra yaḥ śṛṇoti kathām imām|| 35||
mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇam etadakhilaṃ śṛṇvannaśocyaḥ pumān
yo vāsamyagudīrayedrasamayaṃ śocyo na so 'api dvija|
yogajñānaviśuddhasiddhisahitaḥ svargādiloke 'apyasau
śakrādyaiśca surādibhiḥ parivṛtaḥ svarge sadā pūjyate|| 36||
purāṇam etacchrutvā ca jñānavijñānasaṃyutam|
vimānavaram āruhya svargaloke mahīyate|| 37||
purāṇākṣarasaṃkhyā ca prakhyātā tattvabuddhinā|
ślokānaṃ ṣaṭ sahasrāṇi tathā cāṣṭaśatāni ca|| 38||
ślokāstatra navāśītirekādaśasamāhitāḥ|
kathitā muninā pūrvaṃ mārkaṇḍeyena dhīmatā|| 39||
jaiminiruvāca|
bhāratenābhavadyanme saṃśayasphoṭanaṃ dvijāḥ|
tadbhavadbhiḥ kṛtaṃ yanna kaścidadya kariṣyati|| 40||
yūyaṃ dīrghāyuṣyaḥ syāta prajñābuddhiviśāradāḥ|
sāṃkhyayoge tathā cāstu buddhiravyabhicāriṇi|| 41||
pitṛśāpakṛtādduḥkhāddaurmanasyaṃ vyapaitu vaḥ|
etāvaduktvā vacanaṃ jagāma svāśramaṃ muniḥ|
cintayan paramodāraṃ pakṣiṇāṃ vākyam īritam|| 42||
iti śrīmārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe etatpurāṇamāhātmyaśravaṇapaṭhanaphalaṃ nāma catustriṃśadadhikaśatatamo 'adhyāyaḥ|| 134|
sampūrṇam idaṃ mārkaṇḍeyamahāpurāṇam||
śrīśaḥ pāyāt||
